Experiments on three long-context MHQA benchmarks (HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue) and a counterfactual multi-hop dataset demonstrate SIC’s superiority, achieving state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing computational overhead.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 大型语言模型 (LLM) 的最新进展在复杂推理任务（例如数学问题解决和代码生成）中展现了卓越的能力。然而，长上下文中的多跳问答（MHQA）仍然是一个重大挑战，它需要强大的知识密集型推理和对长文档的高效处理。现有的方法通常很难平衡这些要求，要么忽略显式推理，要么由于长上下文中的完全注意机制而产生昂贵的计算成本。为了解决这个问题，我们提出**上下文搜索（SIC）**，这是一种新颖的框架，它将蒙特卡罗树搜索（MCTS）与动态键值（KV）检索相集成，以实现迭代、上下文感知推理。 SIC 在每一步动态检索关键 KV 对（例如 4K 令牌），优先考虑相关证据，同时缓解“中间丢失”问题。此外，本文还引入了一种在自动标记数据上训练的过程奖励模型（PRM），以逐步奖励的方式指导 MCTS 过程，从而在无需手动注释的情况下促进高质量的推理轨迹。对三个长上下文 MHQA 基准（HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA、MuSiQue）和反事实多跳数据集的实验证明了 SIC 的优越性，实现了最先进的性能，同时显着降低了计算开销。

---

## 214. Training Language Model to Critique for Better Refinement

**类别**: `Findings`

**作者**: Tianshu Yu, Chao Xiang, Mingchuan Yang, Pei Ke, Bosi Wen, Cunxiang Wang, Jiale Cheng, Li Zhang, Xinyu Mu, Chuxiong Sun, Minlie Huang

**ID**: 2025.findings-acl.1373

**摘要(英文)**:
> Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable evaluation and critique capabilities, providing insightful feedback and identifying flaws in various tasks. However, limited research has explored which types of critiques are most effective for improving model responses or how to generate such critiques. To address this gap, we introduce Refinement-oriented Critique Optimization (RCO), a novel framework designed to train critic models using refinement signals. RCO uses a feedback loop where critiques, generated by the critic model, guide the actor model in refining its responses. The critique utility (CU) quantifies the effectiveness of these refinements, serving as the reward signal for training the critic model. By focusing on critiques that lead to better refinements, RCO eliminates the need for direct critique preference assessment, ensuring that critiques driving meaningful improvements are rewarded. We evaluate RCO across five tasks—dialog generation, summarization, question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation—and show that it significantly outperforms traditional methods and open-source models in terms of critique quality and refinement outcomes. Our contributions include the introduction of RCO, a novel supervision scheme based on refined response preferences, and comprehensive experimental results that highlight the method’s effectiveness in enhancing LLM critique-refinement loops. Code and data will be publicly available upon acceptance of this paper.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 大型语言模型 (LLM) 表现出了卓越的评估和批评能力，可以提供富有洞察力的反馈并识别各种任务中的缺陷。然而，有限的研究探索了哪些类型的批评对于改善模型响应最有效或如何产生此类批评。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了面向细化的批评优化（RCO），这是一种新颖的框架，旨在使用细化信号来训练批评模型。 RCO 使用反馈循环，其中批评者模型生成的批评指导参与者模型完善其响应。批评效用（CU）量化这些改进的有效性，作为训练批评模型的奖励信号。通过专注于带来更好改进的批评，RCO 消除了直接批评偏好评估的需要，确保推动有意义改进的批评得到奖励。我们在五个任务（对话生成、摘要、问题回答、数学推理和代码生成）中评估了 RCO，并表明它在批评质量和细化结果方面显着优于传统方法和开源模型。我们的贡献包括引入 RCO，一种基于细化响应偏好的新颖监督方案，以及强调该方法在增强 LLM 批判-细化循环方面的有效性的综合实验结果。代码和数据将在本文被接受后公开。

---

## 215. DynaQuest: A Dynamic Question Answering Dataset Reflecting Real-World Knowledge Updates

**类别**: `Findings`

**作者**: Qian Lin, Junyi Li, Hwee Tou Ng

**ID**: 2025.findings-acl.1380

**摘要(英文)**:
> The rapidly changing nature of real-world information presents challenges for large language models (LLMs), which are typically trained on static datasets. This limitation makes it difficult for LLMs to accurately perform tasks that require up-to-date knowledge, such as time-sensitive question answering (QA). In this paper, we introduce **DynaQuest**, a **Dyna**mic **Quest**ion answering dataset reflecting knowledge updates in the real world. DynaQuest is based on Wikipedia Infoboxes, which are frequently updated to reflect real-world changes. Our dataset is created by automatically identifying and comparing changes between different versions of Wikipedia pages and generating question-answer pairs based on these updates. To address the challenges posed by our dynamic dataset, we propose **CARL**, a **C**ontext-**A**ware **R**einforcement **L**earning framework to improve the performance of LLMs on time-sensitive question answering. We conduct experiments on our collected dataset across recent time periods and demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, we maintain a dynamic knowledge updating process, providing a periodically evolving benchmark to continually evaluate LLMs’ ability to answer time-sensitive questions.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 现实世界信息的快速变化给大型语言模型 (LLM) 带来了挑战，大型语言模型通常在静态数据集上进行训练。这种限制使得大语言模型很难准确地执行需要最新知识的任务，例如时间敏感的问答（QA）。在本文中，我们介绍**DynaQuest**，一个反映现实世界知识更新的**动态**问答数据集。 DynaQuest 基于维基百科信息框，该信息框经常更新以反映现实世界的变化。我们的数据集是通过自动识别和比较不同版本的维基百科页面之间的更改并根据这些更新生成问答对来创建的。为了解决动态数据集带来的挑战，我们提出**CARL**，这是一个**C**ontext-**A**ware **R**强化**L**学习框架，以提高大语言模型在时间敏感问题回答方面的表现。我们对最近一段时间内收集的数据集进行了实验，并证明了我们方法的有效性。此外，我们保持动态的知识更新过程，提供定期发展的基准来持续评估大语言模型回答时间敏感问题的能力。

---

## 216. Challenging MultimodalLLMs withAfrican Standardized Exams: A DocumentVQAEvaluation

**类别**: `Workshop-AfricaNLP`

**作者**: Victor Tolulope Olufemi, Oreoluwa Boluwatife Babatunde, Emmanuel Bolarinwa, Kausar Yetunde Moshood

**ID**: 2025.africanlp-1.22

**摘要(英文)**:
> Despite rapid advancements in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), their ability to process low-resource African languages in document-based visual question answering (VQA) tasks remains limited. This paper evaluates three state-of-the-art MLLMs—GPT-4o, Claude-3.5 Haiku, and Gemini-1.5 Pro—on WAEC/NECO standardized exam questions in Yoruba, Igbo, and Hausa. We curate a dataset of multiple-choice questions from exam images and compare model accuracies across two prompting strategies: (1) using English prompts for African language questions, and (2) using native-language prompts. While GPT-4o achieves over 90% accuracy for English, performance drops below 40% for African languages, highlighting severe data imbalance in model training. Notably, native-language prompting improves accuracy for most models, yet no system approaches human-level performance, which reaches over 50% in Yoruba, Igbo, and Hausa. These findings emphasize the need for diverse training data, fine-tuning, and dedicated benchmarks that address the linguistic intricacies of African languages in multimodal tasks, paving the way for more equitable and effective AI systems in education.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLM）取得了快速进步，但它们在基于文档的视觉问答（VQA）任务中处理资源匮乏的非洲语言的能力仍然有限。本文针对约鲁巴语、伊博语和豪萨语的 WAEC/NECO 标准化考试问题评估了三种最先进的 MLLM（GPT-4o、Claude-3.5 Haiku 和 Gemini-1.5 Pro）。我们从考试图像中整理了多项选择题的数据集，并比较了两种提示策略的模型准确性：（1）对非洲语言问题使用英语提示，以及（2）使用母语提示。虽然 GPT-4o 对英语的准确率达到了 90% 以上，但对非洲语言的准确率却下降到了 40% 以下，凸显了模型训练中数据严重不平衡的情况。值得注意的是，母语提示提高了大多数模型的准确性，但没有系统达到人类水平的表现，约鲁巴语、伊博语和豪萨语的表现达到了 50% 以上。这些发现强调需要多样化的训练数据、微调和专门的基准来解决多模式任务中非洲语言的复杂性，为教育领域更公平、更有效的人工智能系统铺平道路。

---

## 217. Challenges forAIin MultimodalSTEMAssessments: a Human-AIComparison

**类别**: `Workshop-BEA`

**作者**: Aymeric de Chillaz, Anna Sotnikova, Patrick Jermann, Antoine Bosselut

**ID**: 2025.bea-1.22

**摘要(英文)**:
> Generative AI systems have rapidly advanced, with multimodal input capabilities enabling reasoning beyond text-based tasks. In education, these advancements could influence assessment design and question answering, presenting both opportunities and challenges. To investigate these effects, we introduce a high-quality dataset of 201 university-level STEM questions, manually annotated with features such as image type, role, problem complexity, and question format. Our study analyzes how these features affect generative AI performance compared to students. We evaluate four model families with five prompting strategies, comparing results to the average of 546 student responses per question. Although the best model correctly answers on average 58.5% of the questions using majority vote aggregation, human participants consistently outperform AI on questions involving visual components. Interestingly, human performance remains stable across question features but varies by subject, whereas AI performance is susceptible to both subject matter and question features. Finally, we provide actionable insights for educators, demonstrating how question design can enhance academic integrity by leveraging features that challenge current AI systems without increasing the cognitive burden for students

**摘要(中文)**:
> 生成式人工智能系统迅速发展，其多模式输入功能使推理超越基于文本的任务。在教育领域，这些进步可能会影响评估设计和问题解答，带来机遇和挑战。为了研究这些影响，我们引入了包含 201 个大学级 STEM 问题的高质量数据集，并手动注释了图像类型、角色、问题复杂性和问题格式等特征。我们的研究分析了与学生相比，这些特征如何影响生成式人工智能的表现。我们使用五种提示策略评估了四个模范家庭，并将结果与​​每个问题 546 名学生的平均回答进行比较。尽管最佳模型使用多数投票聚合平均正确回答了 58.5% 的问题，但人类参与者在涉及视觉组件的问题上始终优于人工智能。有趣的是，人类的表现在不同问题特征上保持稳定，但因主题而异，而人工智能的表现则容易受到主题和问题特征的影响。最后，我们为教育工作者提供了可行的见解，展示了问题设计如何通过利用挑战当前人工智能系统的功能而不增加学生的认知负担来增强学术完整性

---

## 218. Overview of theClinIQLink 2025 Shared Task on Medical Question-Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Brandon Colelough, Davis Bartels, Dina Demner-Fushman

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-1.32

**摘要(英文)**:
> In this paper, we present an overview of CLINIQLINK a shared task, collocated with the 24th BioNLP workshop at ACL 2025, designed to stress-test large language models (LLMs) on medically-oriented question answering aimed at the level of a General Practitioner. The challenge supplies 4 978 expert-verified, medical source-grounded question–answer pairs that cover seven formats - true/false, multiple choice, unordered list, short answer, short-inverse, multi-hop, and multi-hop-inverse. Participating systems, bundled in Docker or Apptainer images, are executed on the CodaBench platform or the University of Maryland’s Zaratan cluster. An automated harness (Task 1) scores closed-ended items by exact match and open-ended items with a three-tier embedding metric. A subsequent physician panel (Task 2) audits the top model responses.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 在本文中，我们概述了 CLINIQLINK 的一项共享任务，该任务与 ACL 2025 上的第 24 届 BioNLP 研讨会同期举行，旨在对针对全科医生水平的面向医学的问答的大型语言模型 (LLM) 进行压力测试。该挑战赛提供了 4 978 个经过专家验证、基于医学来源的问答对，涵盖七种格式 - 对/错、多项选择、无序列表、简答、短逆、多跳和多跳逆。捆绑在 Docker 或 Apptainer 镜像中的参与系统在 CodaBench 平台或马里兰大学的 Zaratan 集群上执行。自动化工具（任务 1）通过精确匹配对封闭式项目进行评分，并通过三层嵌入指标对开放式项目进行评分。随后的医师小组（任务 2）审核最佳模型响应。

---

## 219. Overview of theArchEHR-QA2025 Shared Task on Grounded Question Answering from Electronic Health Records

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Sarvesh Soni, Soumya Gayen, Dina Demner-Fushman

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-1.34

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper presents an overview of the ArchEHR-QA 2025 shared task, which was organized with the 24th BioNLP Workshop at ACL 2025. The goal of this shared task is to develop automated responses to patients’ questions by generating answers that are grounded in key clinical evidence from patients’ electronic health records (EHRs). A total of 29 teams participated in the task, collectively submitting 75 systems, with 24 teams providing their system descriptions. The submitted systems encompassed diverse architectures (including approaches that select the most relevant evidence prior to answer generation), leveraging both proprietary and open-weight large language models, as well as employing various tuning strategies such as fine-tuning and few-shot learning. In this paper, we describe the task setup, the dataset used, the evaluation criteria, and the baseline systems. Furthermore, we summarize the methodologies adopted by participating teams and present a comprehensive evaluation and analysis of the submitted systems.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文概述了 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务，该任务是与 ACL 2025 上的第 24 届 BioNLP 研讨会共同组织的。该共享任务的目标是通过生成基于患者电子健康记录 (EHR) 关键临床证据的答案，开发对患者问题的自动响应。共有 29 个团队参与了该任务，总共提交了 75 个系统，其中 24 个团队提供了系统描述。提交的系统包含不同的架构（包括在生成答案之前选择最相关证据的方法），利用专有和开放权重大型语言模型，以及采用各种调整策略，例如微调和小样本学习。在本文中，我们描述了任务设置、使用的数据集、评估标准和基线系统。此外，我们总结了参赛团队采用的方法，并对提交的系统进行了全面的评估和分析。

---

## 220. ArgHiTZatArchEHR-QA2025: A Two-Step Divide and Conquer Approach to Patient Question Answering for Top Factuality

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Adrian Cuadron Cortes, Aimar Sagasti, Maitane Urruela, Iker De La Iglesia, Ane García Domingo-aldama, Aitziber Atutxa Salazar, Josu Goikoetxea, Ander Barrena

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.1

**摘要(英文)**:
> This work presents three different approaches to address the ArchEHR-QA 2025 Shared Task on automated patient question answering. We introduce an end-to-end prompt-based baseline and two two-step methods to divide the task, without utilizing any external knowledge. Both two step approaches first extract essential sentences from the clinical text—by prompt or similarity ranking—, and then generate the final answer from these notes. Results indicate that the re-ranker based two-step system performs best, highlighting the importance of selecting the right approach for each subtask. Our best run achieved an overall score of 0.44, ranking 8th out of 30 on the leaderboard, securing the top position in overall factuality.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 这项工作提出了三种不同的方法来解决有关自动患者问答的 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务。我们引入了基于端到端提示的基线和两种两步方法来划分任务，而无需利用任何外部知识。这两种两步方法首先通过提示或相似性排名从临床文本中提取基本句子，然后从这些注释中生成最终答案。结果表明，基于重新排序的两步系统表现最佳，凸显了为每个子任务选择正确方法的重要性。我们的最好成绩是总分 0.44，在排行榜上的 30 名中排名第 8，在整体真实性方面稳居第一。

---

## 221. UNIBUC-SDatArchEHR-QA2025: Prompting Our Way to ClinicalQAwith Multi-Model Ensembling

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Dragos Ghinea, Ștefania Rîncu

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.2

**摘要(英文)**:
> In response to the ArchEHR-QA 2025 shared task, we present an efficient approach to patient question answering using small, pre-trained models that are widely available to the research community. Our method employs multi-prompt ensembling with models such as Gemma and Mistral, generating binary relevance judgments for clinical evidence extracted from electronic health records (EHRs). We use two distinct prompts (A and B) to assess the relevance of paragraphs to a patient’s question and aggregate the model outputs via a majority vote ensemble. The relevant passages are then summarized using a third prompt (C) with Gemma. By leveraging off-the-shelf models and consumer-grade hardware (1x RTX 5090), we demonstrate that it is possible to improve performance without relying on resource-intensive fine-tuning or training. Additionally, we explore the impact of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and compare the performance of specialized versus general-purpose models, showing that significant improvements can be achieved through effective use of existing models.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 为了响应 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务，我们提出了一种使用研究界广泛使用的小型预训练模型来回答患者问题的有效方法。我们的方法采用与 Gemma 和 Mistral 等模型的多提示集成，为从电子健康记录 (EHR) 中提取的临床证据生成二元相关性判断。我们使用两个不同的提示（A 和 B）来评估段落与患者问题的相关性，并通过多数投票集合聚合模型输出。然后与 Gemma 使用第三个提示 (C) 总结相关段落。通过利用现成的模型和消费级硬件（1x RTX 5090），我们证明可以在不依赖资源密集型微调或培训的情况下提高性能。此外，我们还探讨了思维链 (CoT) 提示的影响，并比较了专用模型与通用模型的性能，表明通过有效使用现有模型可以实现显着的改进。

---

## 222. WisPerMed atArchEHR-QA2025: A Modular, Relevance-First Approach for Grounded Question Answering on Eletronic Health Records

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Jan-Henning Büns, Hendrik Damm, Tabea Pakull, Felix Nensa, Elisabeth Livingstone

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.5

**摘要(英文)**:
> Automatically answering patient questions based on electronic health records (EHRs) requires systems that both identify relevant evidence and generate accurate, grounded responses. We present a three-part pipeline developed by WisPerMed for the ArchEHR-QA 2025 shared task. First, a fine-tuned BioClinicalBERT model classifies note sentences by their relevance using synonym-based and paraphrased data augmentation. Second, a constrained generation step uses DistilBART-MedSummary to produce faithful answers strictly limited to top-ranked evidence. Third, we align each answer sentence to its supporting evidence via BiomedBERT embeddings and ROUGE-based similarity scoring to ensure citation transparency. Our system achieved a 35.0% overall score on the hidden test set, outperforming the organizer’s baseline by 4.3 percentage points. Gains in BERTScore (+44%) and SARI (+119%) highlight substantial improvements in semantic accuracy and relevance. This modular approach demonstrates that enforcing evidence-awareness and citation grounding enhances both answer quality and trustworthiness in clinical QA systems.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 根据电子健康记录 (EHR) 自动回答患者问题需要系统能够识别相关证据并生成准确、有依据的响应。我们提出了 WisPerMed 为 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务开发的由三部分组成的流程。首先，经过微调的 BioClinicalBERT 模型使用基于同义词和释义的数据增强，根据注释句子的相关性对注释句子进行分类。其次，受限生成步骤使用 DistilBART-MedSummary 生成严格限于顶级证据的忠实答案。第三，我们通过 BiomedBERT 嵌入和基于 ROUGE 的相似性评分将每个答案句子与其支持证据对齐，以确保引文透明度。我们的系统在隐藏测试集上取得了 35.0% 的总分，比组织者的基线高出 4.3 个百分点。 BERTcore (+44%) 和 SARI (+119%) 的增长凸显了语义准确性和相关性的显着改进。这种模块化方法表明，加强证据意识和引文基础可以提高临床质量保证系统的答案质量和可信度。

---

## 223. UniBuc-SBatArchEHR-QA2025: A Resource-Constrained Pipeline for Relevance Classification and Grounded Answer Synthesis

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Sebastian Balmus, Dura Bogdan, Ana Sabina Uban

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.7

**摘要(英文)**:
> We describe the UniBuc-SB submission to the ArchEHR-QA shared task, which involved generating grounded answers to patient questions based on electronic health records. Our system exceeded the performance of the provided baseline, achieving higher performance in generating contextually relevant responses. Notably, we developed our approach under constrained computational resources, utilizing only a single NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU. We refrained from incorporating any external datasets, relying solely on the limited training data supplied by the organizers. To address the challenges posed by the low-resource setting, we leveraged off-the-shelf pre-trained language models and fine-tuned them minimally, aiming to maximize performance while minimizing overfitting.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们描述了 UniBuc-SB 提交给 ArchEHR-QA 共享任务的情况，其中涉及根据电子健康记录生成患者问题的接地答案。我们的系统超出了所提供基准的性能，在生成上下文相关响应方面实现了更高的性能。值得注意的是，我们在计算资源有限的情况下开发了我们的方法，仅利用单个 NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU。我们没有合并任何外部数据集，仅依赖组织者提供的有限训练数据。为了解决资源匮乏环境带来的挑战，我们利用现成的预训练语言模型并对它们进行最小程度的微调，旨在最大限度地提高性能，同时最大限度地减少过度拟合。

---

## 224. KRLabs atArchEHR-QA2025: A Verbatim Approach for Evidence-Based Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Adam Kovacs, Paul Schmitt, Gabor Recski

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.8

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present a lightweight, domain‐agnostic verbatim pipeline for evidence‐grounded question answering. Our pipeline operates in two steps: first, a sentence-level extractor flags relevant note sentences using either zero-shot LLM prompts or supervised ModernBERT classifiers. Next, an LLM drafts a question-specific template, which is filled verbatim with sentences from the extraction step. This prevents hallucinations and ensures traceability. In the ArchEHR‐QA 2025 shared task, our system scored 42.01%, ranking top‐10 in core metrics and outperforming the organiser’s 70B‐parameter Llama‐3.3 baseline. We publicly release our code and inference scripts under an MIT license.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们提出了一个轻量级的、与领域无关的逐字管道，用于基于证据的问答。我们的管道分两个步骤运行：首先，句子级提取器使用零样本 LLM 提示或有监督的 ModernBERT 分类器标记相关注释句子。接下来，大语言模型起草一个特定于问题的模板，该模板逐字填充了提取步骤中的句子。这可以防止幻觉并确保可追溯性。在 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务中，我们的系统得分为 42.01%，在核心指标中排名前 10，并且优于组织者的 70B 参数 Llama-3.3 基线。我们根据 MIT 许可证公开发布我们的代码和推理脚本。

---

## 225. LAILab atArchEHR-QA2025: Test-time scaling for evidence selection in grounded question answering from electronic health records

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Tuan Dung Le, Thanh Duong, Shohreh Haddadan, Behzad Jazayeri, Brandon Manley, Thanh Thieu

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.9

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper presents our approach to the ArchEHR shared task on generating answers to real-world patient questions grounded in evidence from electronic health records (EHRs). We investigate the zero-shot capabilities of general-purpose, domain-agnostic large language models (LLMs) in two key aspects: identifying essential supporting evidence and producing concise, coherent answers. To this aim, we propose a two-stage pipeline: (1) evidence identification via test-time scaling (TTS) and (2) generating the final answer conditioned on selected evidences from the previous stage.Our approach leverages high-temperature sampling to generate multiple outputs during the evidence selection phase. This TTS-based approach effectively explore more potential evidences which results in significant improvement of the factuality score of the answers.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文介绍了我们执行 ArchEHR 共享任务的方法，即根据电子健康记录 (EHR) 的证据生成现实世界患者问题的答案。我们从两个关键方面研究了通用的、与领域无关的大型语言模型 (LLM) 的零样本能力：识别必要的支持证据并生成简洁、连贯的答案。为此，我们提出了一个两阶段的管道：（1）通过测试时间缩放（TTS）进行证据识别，（2）根据前一阶段选定的证据生成最终答案。我们的方法利用高温采样在证据选择阶段生成多个输出。这种基于 TTS 的方法有效地探索了更多潜在证据，从而显着提高了答案的真实性得分。

---

## 226. UTSA-NLPatArchEHR-QA2025: ImprovingEHRQuestion Answering via Self-Consistency Prompting

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Sara Shields-Menard, Zach Reimers, Joshua Gardner, David Perry, Anthony Rios

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.10

**摘要(英文)**:
> We describe our system for the ArchEHR-QA Shared Task on answering clinical questions using electronic health records (EHRs). Our approach uses large language models in two steps: first, to find sentences in the EHR relevant to a clinician’s question, and second, to generate a short, citation-supported response based on those sentences. We use few-shot prompting, self-consistency, and thresholding to improve the sentence classification step to decide which sentences are essential. We compare several models and find that a smaller 8B model performs better than a larger 70B model for identifying relevant information. Our results show that accurate sentence selection is critical for generating high-quality responses and that self-consistency with thresholding helps make these decisions more reliable.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们描述了我们的 ArchEHR-QA 共享任务系统，该系统使用电子健康记录 (EHR) 回答临床问题。我们的方法分两步使用大型语言模型：首先，在 EHR 中查找与临床医生问题相关的句子，其次，根据这些句子生成简短的、有引用支持的回复。我们使用少样本提示、自洽和阈值来改进句子分类步骤，以决定哪些句子是必要的。我们比较了几个模型，发现较小的 8B 模型比较大的 70B 模型在识别相关信息方面表现更好。我们的结果表明，准确的句子选择对于生成高质量的响应至关重要，并且阈值的自我一致性有助于使这些决策更加可靠。

---

## 227. UTSamuel atArchEHR-QA2025: A Clinical Question Answering System for Responding to Patient Portal Messages Using GenerativeAI

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Samuel Reason, Liwei Wang, Hongfang Liu, Ming Huang

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.11

**摘要(英文)**:
> Responding to patient portal messages places a substantial burden on clinicians. To mitigate this, automatically generating answers to patient questions by considering their medical records is a critical solution. In this study, we proposed a clinical question answering system for the BioNLP 2025 Shared Task on Grounded Electronic Health Record Question Answering. The system processed each patient message case by selecting relevant sentences as evidences from the associated clinical notes and generating a concise, medically accurate answer to the patient’s question. A generative AI model from OpenAI (GPT-4o) was leveraged to assist with sentence selection and answer generation. Each response is grounded in source text, limited to 75 words, and includes sentence-level citations. The system was evaluated on 100 test cases using alignment, citation, and summarization metrics. Our results indicate the significant potential of the clinical question answering system based on generative AI models to streamline communication between patients and healthcare providers by automatically generating responses to patient messages.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 响应患者门户消息给临床医生带来了沉重的负担。为了缓解这种情况，通过考虑患者的医疗记录自动生成患者问题的答案是一个关键的解决方案。在这项研究中，我们为 BioNLP 2025 接地电子健康记录问答共享任务提出了一个临床问答系统。该系统通过从相关临床记录中选择相关句子作为证据来处理每个患者消息案例，并对患者的问题生成简洁、医学上准确的答案。利用 OpenAI (GPT-4o) 的生成式人工智能模型来协助句子选择和答案生成。每个回复均以源文本为基础，限制为 75 个单词，并包含句子级引用。使用对齐、引用和摘要指标对 100 个测试用例对该系统进行了评估。我们的结果表明，基于生成式人工智能模型的临床问答系统具有巨大潜力，可以通过自动生成对患者消息的响应来简化患者和医疗保健提供者之间的沟通。

---

## 228. LAMARatArchEHR-QA2025: Clinically AlignedLLM-Generated Few-Shot Learning forEHR-Grounded Patient Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Seksan Yoadsanit, Nopporn Lekuthai, Watcharitpol Sermsrisuwan, Titipat Achakulvisut

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.12

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper presents an approach to answering patient-specific medical questions using electronic health record (EHR) grounding with ArchEHR-QA 2025 datasets. We address medical question answering as an alignment problem, focusing on generating responses factually consistent with patient-specific clinical notes through in-context learning techniques. We show that LLM-generated responses, used as few-shot examples with GPT-4.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro, significantly outperform baseline approaches (overall score = 49.1), achieving strict precision, recall, and F1-micro scores of 60.6, 53.6, and 56.9, respectively, on the ArchEHR-QA 2025 test leaderboard. It achieves textual similarity between answers and essential evidence using BLEU, ROUGE, SARI, BERTScore, AlignScore, and MEDCON scores of 6.0, 32.1, 65.8, 36.4, 64.3, and 43.6, respectively. Our findings highlight the effectiveness of combining EHR grounding with few-shot examples for personalized medical question answering, establishing a promising approach for developing accurate and personalized medical question answering systems. We release our code at https://github.com/biodatlab/archehr-qa-lamar.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文提出了一种使用基于 ArchEHR-QA 2025 数据集的电子健康记录 (EHR) 来回答患者特定医疗问题的方法。我们将医疗问答视为一个对齐问题，重点是通过上下文学习技术生成与特定患者的临床记录实际上一致的响应。我们表明，LLM 生成的响应（用作 GPT-4.1 和 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的少数样本示例）显着优于基线方法（总分 = 49.1），在 ArchEHR-QA 2025 测试排行榜上分别实现了严格的精确度、召回率和 F1-micro 分数 60.6、53.6 和 56.9。它使用 BLEU、ROUGE、SARI、BERTScore、AlignScore 和 MEDCON 分数分别为 6.0、32.1、65.8、36.4、64.3 和 43.6 来实现答案和基本证据之间的文本相似性。我们的研究结果强调了将 EHR 基础与个性化医疗问答系统的少量示例相结合的有效性，为开发准确和个性化的医疗问答系统建立了一种有前景的方法。我们在 https://github.com/biodatlab/archehr-qa-lamar 发布了我们的代码。

---

## 229. Neural atArchEHR-QA2025: Agentic Prompt Optimization for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Sai Prasanna Teja Reddy Bogireddy, Abrar Majeedi, Viswanath Gajjala, Zhuoyan Xu, Siddhant Rai, Vaishnav Potlapalli

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.13

**摘要(英文)**:
> Automated question answering (QA) over electronic health records (EHRs) can bridge critical information gaps for clinicians and patients, yet it demands both precise evidence retrieval and faithful answer generation under limited supervision. In this work, we present Neural, the runner-up in the BioNLP 2025 ArchEHR-QA shared task on evidence grounded clinical QA. Our proposed method decouples the task into (1) sentence-level evidence identification and (2) answer synthesis with explicit citations. For each stage, we automatically explore the prompt space with DSPy’s MIPROv2 optimizer, jointly tuning instructions and few-shot demonstrations on the development set. A self-consistency voting scheme further improves evidence recall without sacrificing precision. On the hidden test set, our method attains an overall score of 51.5, placing second stage while outperforming standard zero-shot and few-shot prompting by over 20 and 10 points, respectively. These results indicate that data-driven prompt optimization is a cost-effective alternative to model fine-tuning for high-stakes clinical QA, advancing the reliability of AI assistants in healthcare.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 基于电子健康记录 (EHR) 的自动问答 (QA) 可以弥补临床医生和患者之间的关键信息差距，但它需要在有限的监督下进行精确的证据检索和忠实的答案生成。在这项工作中，我们展示了 Neural，它是 BioNLP 2025 ArchEHR-QA 循证临床 QA 共享任务中的亚军。我们提出的方法将任务分解为（1）句子级证据识别和（2）带有显式引用的答案合成。对于每个阶段，我们都会使用 DSPy 的 MIPROv2 优化器自动探索提示空间，联合调整指令并在开发集上进行少量演示。自洽投票方案进一步提高了证据召回率，同时又不牺牲准确性。在隐藏测试集上，我们的方法获得了 51.5 的总分，排名第二，同时比标准零样本提示和少样本提示分别高出 20 分和 10 分以上。这些结果表明，数据驱动的即时优化是高风险临床 QA 模型微调的一种经济高效的替代方案，可提高医疗保健领域人工智能助手的可靠性。

---

## 230. UICatArchEHR-QA2025: Tri-Step Pipeline for Reliable Grounded Medical Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Mohammad Arvan, Anuj Gautam, Mohan Zalake, Karl M. Kochendorfer

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.14

**摘要(英文)**:
> Automated response generation from electronic health records (EHRs) holds potential to reduce clinician workload, but it introduces important challenges related to factual accuracy and reliable grounding in clinical evidence. We present a structured three-step pipeline that uses large language models (LLMs) for evidence classification, guided response generation, and iterative quality control. To enable rigorous evaluation, our framework combines traditional reference-based metrics with a claim-level “LLM-as-a-Judge” methodology. On the ArchEHR-QA benchmark, our system achieves 82.0 percent claim-level evidence faithfulness and 51.6 percent citation-level factuality, demonstrating strong performance in generating clinically grounded responses. These findings highlight the utility of structured LLM pipelines in healthcare applications, while also underscoring the importance of transparent evaluation and continued refinement. All code, prompt templates, and evaluation tools are publicly available.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 从电子健康记录 (EHR) 中自动生成响应有可能减少临床医生的工作量，但它带来了与事实准确性和临床证据可靠基础相关的重要挑战。我们提出了一个结构化的三步管道，它使用大型语言模型（LLM）进行证据分类、引导响应生成和迭代质量控制。为了实现严格的评估，我们的框架将传统的基于参考的指标与索赔级别的“大语言模型作为法官”方法相结合。在 ArchEHR-QA 基准上，我们的系统达到了 82.0% 的声明级证据可信度和 51.6% 的引文级事实性，在生成基于临床的响应方面表现出强大的性能。这些发现凸显了结构化大语言模型管道在医疗保健应用中的实用性，同时也强调了透明评估和持续改进的重要性。所有代码、提示模板和评估工具都是公开可用的。

---

## 231. CogStack-KCL-UCLatArchEHR-QA2025: Investigating HybridLLMApproaches for Grounded Clinical Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Shubham Agarwal, Thomas Searle, Kawsar Noor, Richard Dobson

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.16

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present our system for the ArchEHR shared task, which focuses on answering clinical and patient-facing questions grounded in real-world EHR data. Our core contribution is a 2-Stage prompting pipeline that separates evidence selection from answer generation while employing in-context learning strategies. Our experimentation leveraged the open-weight Gemma-v3 family of models, with our best submission using the Gemma-12B model securing 5th place overall on the unseen test set. Through systematic experimentation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of task decomposition in improving both factual accuracy and answer relevance in grounded clinical question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们展示了用于 ArchEHR 共享任务的系统，该系统侧重于回答基于现实世界 EHR 数据的临床和患者面临的问题。我们的核心贡献是一个两阶段提示管道，它将证据选择与答案生成分开，同时采用上下文学习策略。我们的实验利用了开放权重 Gemma-v3 系列模型，我们使用 Gemma-12B 模型提交的最佳作品在未见过的测试集上总体排名第五。通过系统的实验，我们证明了任务分解在提高扎根临床问答中的事实准确性和答案相关性方面的有效性。

---

## 232. SzegedAIatArchEHR-QA2025: CombiningLLMs with traditional methods for grounded question answering

**类别**: `Workshop-BioNLP`

**作者**: Soma Nagy, Bálint Nyerges, Zsombor Kispéter, Gábor Tóth, András Szlúka, Gábor Kőrösi, Zsolt Szántó, Richárd Farkas

**ID**: 2025.bionlp-share.17

**摘要(英文)**:
> In this paper, we present the SzegedAI team’s submissions to the ArchEHR-QA 2025 shared task. Our approaches include multiple prompting techniques for large language models (LLMs), sentence similarity methods, and traditional feature engineering. We are aiming to explore both modern and traditional solutions to the task. To combine the strengths of these diverse methods, we employed different ensembling strategies.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 在本文中，我们介绍了 SzegedAI 团队向 ArchEHR-QA 2025 共享任务提交的内容。我们的方法包括大型语言模型（LLM）的多种提示技术、句子相似性方法和传统的特征工程。我们的目标是探索解决这一任务的现代和传统解决方案。为了结合这些不同方法的优势，我们采用了不同的集成策略。

---

## 233. Planning for Success: ExploringLLMLong-term Planning Capabilities in Table Understanding

**类别**: `Workshop-CoNLL`

**作者**: Thi-Nhung Nguyen, Hoang Ngo, Dinh Phung, Thuy-Trang Vu, Dat Quoc Nguyen

**ID**: 2025.conll-1.6

**摘要(英文)**:
> Table understanding is key to addressing challenging downstream tasks such as table-based question answering and fact verification. Recent works have focused on leveraging Chain-of-Thought and question decomposition to solve complex questions requiring multiple operations on tables. However, these methods often suffer from a lack of explicit long-term planning and weak inter-step connections, leading to miss constraints within questions. In this paper, we propose leveraging the long-term planning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) to enhance table understanding. Our approach enables the execution of a long-term plan, where the steps are tightly interconnected and serve the ultimate goal, an aspect that methods based on Chain-of-Thought and question decomposition lack. In addition, our method effectively minimizes the inclusion of unnecessary details in the process of solving the next short-term goals, a limitation of methods based on Chain-of-Thought. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on WikiTableQuestions and TabFact datasets.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 表理解是解决具有挑战性的下游任务（例如基于表的问答和事实验证）的关键。最近的工作重点是利用思维链和问题分解来解决需要对表进行多次操作的复杂问题。然而，这些方法往往缺乏明确的长期规划和步骤间连接薄弱，导致问题中遗漏约束。在本文中，我们建议利用大型语言模型（LLM）的长期规划能力来增强表理解。我们的方法能够执行长期计划，其中步骤紧密相连并服务于最终目标，这是基于思维链和问题分解的方法所缺乏的方面。此外，我们的方法有效地减少了解决下一个短期目标过程中不必要的细节，这是基于思维链的方法的局限性。大量实验表明，我们的方法优于强大的基线，并在 WikiTableQuestions 和 TabFact 数据集上实现了最先进的性能。

---

## 234. GQC:LLM-Based GroupedQAConsolidation for Open-Domain Fact Verification atAVeriTeC

**类别**: `Workshop-FEVER`

**作者**: Dongzhuoran Zhou, Roxana Pop, Yuqicheng Zhu, Evgeny Kharlamov

**ID**: 2025.fever-1.11

**摘要(英文)**:
> Structured fact verification benchmarks like AVeriTeC decompose claims into QA pairs to support fine-grained reasoning. However, current systems generate QA pairs independently for each evidence sentence, leading to redundancy, drift, and noise. We introduce a modular LLM-based QA consolidation module that jointly filters, clusters, and rewrites QA pairs at the claim level. Experiments show that this method improves evidence quality and veracity prediction accuracy. Our analysis also highlights the impact of model scale and alignment on downstream performance.

**摘要(中文)**:
> AVeriTeC 等结构化事实验证基准将声明分解为 QA 对，以支持细粒度推理。然而，当前的系统为每个证据句子独立生成问答对，导致冗余、漂移和噪声。我们引入了一个基于 LLM 的模块化 QA 整合模块，该模块在声明级别联合过滤、聚类和重写 QA 对。实验表明，该方法提高了证据质量和真实性预测精度。我们的分析还强调了模型规模和一致性对下游性能的影响。

---

## 235. Graph-of-Thoughts for Fact-Checking with Large Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-FEVER`

**作者**: Sascha Rolinger, Jin Liu

**ID**: 2025.fever-1.21

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present a fact-checking system developed for the 2025 Automated Verification of Textual Claims (AVeriTeC) shared task, leveraging the Graph-of-Thoughts (GoT) prompting scheme. The GoT approach facilitates iterative refinement during fact-checking by conditioningquestion generation on previous answers and enabling the incorporation of multiple evidence documents per question, thereby mitigatingthe impact of factually incorrect evidence. The efficiency requirements of the shared task are addressed by restricting the width and depthof the thought graph. Additionally, an efficient stopping criterion is derived from the dataset’s Not Enough Information (NEI) label. Our system utilizes fine-tuned open-source Large Language Models (LLMs) for question generation, question answering, and final verdict prediction. Empirical results demonstrate competitive performance against top-performing systems in the AVeriTeC shared task and improvements over the baseline method. Our code is publicly available.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们提出了一个为 2025 年文本声明自动验证 (AVeriTeC) 共享任务开发的事实检查系统，利用了思维图 (GoT) 提示方案。 GoT 方法通过根据先前的答案生成问题并允许每个问题合并多个证据文档，从而促进事实检查期间的迭代细化，从而减轻事实上不正确的证据的影响。通过限制思维图的宽度和深度来解决共享任务的效率要求。此外，有效的停止标准源自数据集的信息不足 (NEI) 标签。我们的系统利用经过微调的开源大型语言模型 (LLM) 进行问题生成、问题回答和最终判决预测。实证结果表明，在 AVeriTeC 共享任务中，与表现最佳的系统相比，其性能具有竞争力，并且比基线方法有所改进。我们的代码是公开的。

---

## 236. JBBQ:Japanese Bias Benchmark for Analyzing Social Biases in Large Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-GEBNLP`

**作者**: Hitomi Yanaka, Namgi Han, Ryoma Kumon, Lu Jie, Masashi Takeshita, Ryo Sekizawa, Taisei Katô, Hiromi Arai

**ID**: 2025.gebnlp-1.1

**摘要(英文)**:
> With the development of large language models (LLMs), social biases in these LLMs have become a pressing issue.Although there are various benchmarks for social biases across languages, the extent to which Japanese LLMs exhibit social biases has not been fully investigated.In this study, we construct the Japanese Bias Benchmark dataset for Question Answering (JBBQ) based on the English bias benchmark BBQ, with analysis of social biases in Japanese LLMs.The results show that while current open Japanese LLMs with more parameters show improved accuracies on JBBQ, their bias scores increase.In addition, prompts with a warning about social biases and chain-of-thought prompting reduce the effect of biases in model outputs, but there is room for improvement in extracting the correct evidence from contexts in Japanese. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/ynklab/JBBQ_data.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 随着大语言模型（LLM）的发展，这些LLM中的社会偏见已成为一个紧迫的问题。虽然跨语言的社会偏见有多种基准，但日本LLM表现出社会偏见的程度尚未得到充分调查。在本研究中，我们基于英语偏见基准BBQ构建了日本LLM的社会偏见数据集（JBBQ），并对日本LLM中的社会偏见进行了分析。结果表明，虽然当前开放的日本LLM具有更多参数，但仍表现出社会偏见。 JBBQ 的准确性提高，其偏差分数也会增加。此外，有关社会偏差的警告提示和思维链提示会减少模型输出中偏差的影响，但从日语上下文中提取正确证据方面还有改进的空间。我们的数据集可在 https://github.com/ynklab/JBBQ_data 获取。

---

## 237. Intersectional Bias inJapanese Large Language Models from a Contextualized Perspective

**类别**: `Workshop-GEBNLP`

**作者**: Hitomi Yanaka, Xinqi He, Lu Jie, Namgi Han, Sunjin Oh, Ryoma Kumon, Yuma Matsuoka, Kazuhiko Watabe, Yuko Itatsu

**ID**: 2025.gebnlp-1.2

**摘要(英文)**:
> An growing number of studies have examined the social bias of rapidly developed large language models (LLMs). Although most of these studies have focused on bias occurring in a single social attribute, research in social science has shown that social bias often occurs in the form of intersectionality—the constitutive and contextualized perspective on bias aroused by social attributes. In this study, we construct the Japanese benchmark inter-JBBQ, designed to evaluate the intersectional bias in LLMs on the question-answering setting. Using inter-JBBQ to analyze GPT-4o and Swallow, we find that biased output varies according to its contexts even with the equal combination of social attributes.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 越来越多的研究探讨了快速发展的大型语言模型（LLM）的社会偏见。尽管大多数研究都集中在单一社会属性中发生的偏见，但社会科学研究表明，社会偏见往往以交叉性的形式发生——即对社会属性引起的偏见的构成性和情境化视角。在本研究中，我们构建了日本基准 Inter-JBBQ，旨在评估大语言模型在问答环境中的交叉偏差。使用 inter-JBBQ 来分析 GPT-4o 和 Swallow，我们发现即使社交属性的组合相同，偏差输出也会根据其上下文而变化。

---

## 238. Measuring Gender Bias in Language Models inFarsi

**类别**: `Workshop-GEBNLP`

**作者**: Hamidreza Saffari, Mohammadamin Shafiei, Donya Rooein, Debora Nozza

**ID**: 2025.gebnlp-1.21

**摘要(英文)**:
> As Natural Language Processing models become increasingly embedded in everyday life, ensuring that these systems can measure and mitigate bias is critical. While substantial work has been done to identify and mitigate gender bias in English, Farsi remains largely underexplored. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of gender bias in language models in Farsi across three tasks: emotion analysis, question answering, and hurtful sentence completion. We assess a range of language models across all the tasks in zero-shot settings. By adapting established evaluation frameworks for Farsi, we uncover patterns of gender bias that differ from those observed in English, highlighting the urgent need for culturally and linguistically inclusive approaches to bias mitigation in NLP.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 随着自然语言处理模型越来越融入日常生活，确保这些系统能够测量和减轻偏见至关重要。尽管在识别和减轻英语中的性别偏见方面已经做了大量工作，但波斯语仍然很大程度上未被开发。本文首次对波斯语语言模型中的性别偏见进行了全面研究，涉及三个任务：情感分析、问题回答和伤害性句子完成。我们在零样本设置中评估了所有任务的一系列语言模型。通过调整波斯语的既定评估框架，我们发现了与英语中观察到的性别偏见模式不同的模式，凸显了 NLP 中迫切需要文化和语言包容性的方法来减轻偏见。

---

## 239. Cleanse: Uncertainty Estimation Approach Using Clustering-based Semantic Consistency inLLMs

**类别**: `Workshop-GEM`

**作者**: Minsuh Joo, Hyunsoo Cho

**ID**: 2025.gem-1.25

**摘要(英文)**:
> Despite the outstanding performance of large language models (LLMs) across various NLP tasks, hallucinations in LLMs–where LLMs generate inaccurate responses–remains as a critical problem as it can be directly connected to a crisis of building safe and reliable LLMs. Uncertainty estimation is primarily used to measure hallucination levels in LLM responses so that correct and incorrect answers can be distinguished clearly. This study proposes an effective uncertainty estimation approach, Clustering-based semantic consistency (Cleanse). Cleanse quantifies the uncertainty with the proportion of the intra-cluster consistency in the total consistency between LLM hidden embeddings which contain adequate semantic information of generations, by employing clustering. The effectiveness of Cleanse for detecting hallucination is validated using four off-the-shelf models, LLaMA-7B, LLaMA-13B, LLaMA2-7B and Mistral-7B and two question-answering benchmarks, SQuAD and CoQA.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 尽管大型语言模型（LLM）在各种 NLP 任务中表现出色，但 LLM 中的幻觉（即 LLM 生成不准确的响应）仍然是一个关键问题，因为它可能与构建安全可靠的 LLM 的危机直接相关。不确定性估计主要用于测量大语言模型回答中的幻觉水平，以便可以清楚地区分正确和错误的答案。本研究提出了一种有效的不确定性估计方法，基于聚类的语义一致性（Cleanse）。 Cleanse 通过采用聚类，用包含足够的代语义信息的 LLM 隐藏嵌入之间的总一致性中簇内一致性的比例来量化不确定性。 Cleanse 检测幻觉的有效性通过四个现成模型（LLaMA-7B、LLaMA-13B、LLaMA2-7B 和 Mistral-7B）以及两个问答基准（SQuAD 和 CoQA）进行了验证。

---

## 240. Analyzing the Sensitivity of Vision Language Models in Visual Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-GEM`

**作者**: Monika Shah, Sudarshan Balaji, Somdeb Sarkhel, Sanorita Dey, Deepak Venugopal

**ID**: 2025.gem-1.36

**摘要(英文)**:
> We can think of Visual Question Answering as a (multimodal) conversation between a human and an AI system. Here, we explore the sensitivity of Vision Language Models (VLMs) through the lens of cooperative principles of conversation proposed by Grice. Specifically, even when Grice’s maxims of conversation are flouted, humans typically do not have much difficulty in understanding the conversation even though it requires more cognitive effort. Here, we study if VLMs are capable of handling violations to Grice’s maxims in a manner that is similar to humans. Specifically, we add modifiers to human-crafted questions and analyze the response of VLMs to these modifiers. We use three state-of-the-art VLMs in our study, namely, GPT-4o, Claude-3.5-Sonnet and Gemini-1.5-Flash on questions from the VQA v2.0 dataset. Our initial results seem to indicate that the performance of VLMs consistently diminish with the addition of modifiers which indicates our approach as a promising direction to understand the limitations of VLMs.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们可以将视觉问答视为人类和人工智能系统之间的（多模式）对话。在这里，我们通过 Grice 提出的对话合作原则来探索视觉语言模型 (VLM) 的敏感性。具体来说，即使格赖斯的对话格言遭到蔑视，人类通常在理解对话方面也没有太大困难，尽管它需要更多的认知努力。在这里，我们研究 VLM 是否能够以类似于人类的方式处理违反 Grice 准则的行为。具体来说，我们为人工设计的问题添加修饰符，并分析 VLM 对这些修饰符的响应。我们在研究中使用了三种最先进的 VLM，即 GPT-4o、Claude-3.5-Sonnet 和 Gemini-1.5-Flash 来处理 VQA v2.0 数据集的问题。我们的初步结果似乎表明，VLM 的性能随着修饰符的添加而持续下降，这表明我们的方法是了解 VLM 局限性的一个有希望的方向。

---

## 241. Quantifying Memorization and Parametric Response Rates in Retrieval-Augmented Vision-Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-L2M2`

**作者**: Peter Carragher, Abhinand Jha, Raghav R, Kathleen M. Carley

**ID**: 2025.l2m2-1.10

**摘要(英文)**:
> Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities in question answering (QA), but metrics for assessing their reliance on memorization versus retrieval remain underdeveloped. Moreover, while finetuned models are state-of-the-art on closed-domain tasks, general-purpose models like GPT-4o exhibit strong zero-shot performance. This raises questions about the trade-offs between memorization, generalization, and retrieval. In this work, we analyze the extent to which multimodal retrieval-augmented VLMs memorize training data compared to baseline VLMs. Using the WebQA benchmark, we contrast finetuned models with baseline VLMs on multihop retrieval and question answering, examining the impact of finetuning on data memorization. To quantify memorization in end-to-end retrieval and QA systems, we propose several proxy metrics by investigating instances where QA succeeds despite retrieval failing. In line with existing work, we find that finetuned models rely more heavily on memorization than retrieval-augmented VLMs, and achieve higher accuracy as a result (72% vs 52% on WebQA test set). Finally, we present the first empirical comparison of the parametric effect between text and visual modalities. Here, we find that image-based questions have parametric response rates that are consistently 15-25% higher than for text-based questions in the WebQA dataset. As such, our measures pose a challenge for future work, both to account for differences in model memorization across different modalities and more generally to reconcile memorization and generalization in joint Retrieval-QA tasks.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 大型语言模型 (LLM) 在问答 (QA) 方面表现出卓越的能力，但评估其对记忆与检索的依赖程度的指标仍然不发达。此外，虽然微调模型在闭域任务上是最先进的，但像 GPT-4o 这样的通用模型却表现出了强大的零样本性能。这就提出了关于记忆、泛化和检索之间权衡的问题。在这项工作中，我们分析了多模态检索增强 VLM 与基线 VLM 相比记忆训练数据的程度。使用 WebQA 基准，我们将微调模型与多跳检索和问答方面的基线 VLM 进行对比，检查微调对数据记忆的影响。为了量化端到端检索和 QA 系统中的记忆，我们通过调查尽管检索失败但 QA 成功的实例，提出了几个代理指标。与现有工作一致，我们发现微调模型比检索增强的 VLM 更依赖于记忆，并因此获得更高的准确率（WebQA 测试集上为 72% vs 52%）。最后，我们首次对文本和视觉方式之间的参数效果进行了实证比较。在这里，我们发现 WebQA 数据集中基于图像的问题的参数响应率始终比基于文本的问题高 15-25%。因此，我们的措施对未来的工作提出了挑战，既要解释不同模式之间模型记忆的差异，更广泛地要协调联合检索-QA 任务中的记忆和泛化。

---

## 242. Robust Data Watermarking in Language Models by Injecting Fictitious Knowledge

**类别**: `Workshop-L2M2`

**作者**: Xinyue Cui, Johnny Wei, Swabha Swayamdipta, Robin Jia

**ID**: 2025.l2m2-1.15

**摘要(英文)**:
> Data watermarking in language models injects traceable signals, such as specific token sequences or stylistic patterns, into copyrighted text, allowing copyright holders to track and verify training data ownership. Previous data watermarking techniques primarily focus on effective memorization during pretraining, while overlooking challenges that arise in other stages of the LLM lifecycle, such as the risk of watermark filtering during data preprocessing and verification difficulties due to API-only access. To address these challenges, we propose a novel data watermarking approach that injects plausible yet fictitious knowledge into training data using generated passages describing a fictitious entity and its associated attributes. Our watermarks are designed to be memorized by the LLM through seamlessly integrating in its training data, making them harder to detect lexically during preprocessing. We demonstrate that our watermarks can be effectively memorized by LLMs, and that increasing our watermarks’ density, length, and diversity of attributes strengthens their memorization. We further show that our watermarks remain effective after continual pretraining and supervised finetuning. Finally, we show that our data watermarks can be evaluated even under API-only access via question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 语言模型中的数据水印将可追踪的信号（例如特定的标记序列或文体模式）注入受版权保护的文本中，从而允许版权所有者跟踪和验证训练数据的所有权。以往的数据水印技术主要关注预训练期间的有效记忆，而忽略了LLM生命周期其他阶段出现的挑战，例如数据预处理期间水印过滤的风险以及仅API访问导致的验证困难。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的数据水印方法，该方法使用生成的描述虚构实体及其相关属性的段落将合理但虚构的知识注入训练数据中。我们的水印旨在通过无缝集成到其训练数据中而被大语言模型记住，从而使它们在预处理过程中更难以从词汇上检测到。我们证明了大语言模型可以有效地记住我们的水印，并且增加水印的密度、长度和属性的多样性可以增强他们的记忆。我们进一步表明，在持续的预训练和监督微调后，我们的水印仍然有效。最后，我们展示了即使在仅通过问答访问 API 的情况下也可以评估我们的数据水印。

---

## 243. Safe in Isolation, Dangerous Together: Agent-Driven Multi-Turn Decomposition Jailbreaks onLLMs

**类别**: `Workshop-ReALM`

**作者**: Devansh Srivastav, Xiao Zhang

**ID**: 2025.realm-1.13

**摘要(英文)**:
> Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in critical domains, but their vulnerability to jailbreak attacks remains a significant concern. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent, multi-turn jailbreak strategy that systematically bypasses LLM safety mechanisms by decomposing harmful queries into seemingly benign sub-tasks. Built upon a role-based agentic framework consisting of a Question Decomposer, a Sub-Question Answerer, and an Answer Combiner, we demonstrate how LLMs can be manipulated to generate prohibited content without prompt manipulations. Our results show a drastic increase in attack success, often exceeding 90% across various LLMs, including GPT-3.5-Turbo, Gemma-2-9B, and Mistral-7B. We further analyze attack consistency across multiple runs and vulnerability across content categories. Compared to existing widely used jailbreak techniques, our multi-agent method consistently achieves the highest attack success rate across all evaluated models. These findings reveal a critical flaw in the current safety architecture of multi-agent LLM systems: their lack of holistic context awareness. By revealing this weakness, we argue for an urgent need to develop multi-turn, context-aware, and robust defenses to address this emerging threat vector.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 大型语言模型 (LLM) 越来越多地部署在关键领域，但它们易受越狱攻击的脆弱性仍然是一个重大问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种多代理、多轮越狱策略，通过将有害查询分解为看似良性的子任务，系统地绕过 LLM 安全机制。基于由问题分解器、子问题解答器和答案组合器组成的基于角色的代理框架，我们演示了如何操纵 LLM 来生成禁止的内容，而无需进行提示操作。我们的结果显示，各种 LLM 的攻击成功率大幅提高，通常超过 90%，包括 GPT-3.5-Turbo、Gemma-2-9B 和 Mistral-7B。我们进一步分析多次运行的攻击一致性和跨内容类别的漏洞。与现有广泛使用的越狱技术相比，我们的多代理方法在所有评估的模型中始终实现了最高的攻击成功率。这些发现揭示了当前多代理 LLM 系统安全架构的一个关键缺陷：缺乏整体情境意识。通过揭示这一弱点，我们认为迫切需要开发多回合、上下文感知和强大的防御措施来应对这一新兴的威胁媒介。

---

## 244. VisTRA: Visual Tool-use Reasoning Analyzer for Small Object Visual Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-ReALM`

**作者**: Hiroaki Sugiyama, Ko Koga, Toshifumi Nishijima

**ID**: 2025.realm-1.26

**摘要(英文)**:
> This study proposes VisTRA (Visual Tool-use Reasoning Analyzer), a framework for analyzing how Visual Language Models (VLMs) utilize tools in VQA tasks involving small objects in high-resolution images. While tools like object detection and zoom functionality are essential for small object VQA, their potential errors necessitate careful verification of outputs. Our framework provides systematic evaluation of VLMs’ tool-use capabilities through analysis of verification patterns. Using the V* bench dataset, we find that direct acceptance of tool outputs correlates with decreased VQA accuracy, while lower-performing models exhibit higher frequencies of cyclic verification loops. These findings offer insights for improving tool verification mechanisms in VLM architectures focused on small object detection tasks.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本研究提出了 VisTRA（视觉工具使用推理分析器），这是一个框架，用于分析视觉语言模型 (VLM) 如何在涉及高分辨率图像中的小物体的 VQA 任务中利用工具。虽然对象检测和缩放功能等工具对于小对象 VQA 至关重要，但它们的潜在错误需要仔细验证输出。我们的框架通过分析验证模式对 VLM 的工具使用能力进行系统评估。使用 V* 基准数据集，我们发现直接接受工具输出与 VQA 准确性下降相关，而性能较低的模型表现出较高的循环验证循环频率。这些发现为改进专注于小物体检测任务的 VLM 架构中的工具验证机制提供了见解。

---

## 245. SciVQA2025: Overview of the First Scientific Visual Question Answering Shared Task

**类别**: `Workshop-SDP`

**作者**: Ekaterina Borisova, Nikolas Rauscher, Georg Rehm

**ID**: 2025.sdp-1.18

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper provides an overview of the First Scientific Visual Question Answering (SciVQA) shared task conducted as part of the Fifth Scholarly Document Processing workshop (SDP 2025). SciVQA aims to explore the capabilities of current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in reasoning over figures from scholarly publications for question answering (QA). The main focus of the challenge is on closed-ended visual and non-visual QA pairs. We developed the novel SciVQA benchmark comprising 3,000 images of figures and a total of 21,000 QA pairs. The shared task received seven submissions, with the best performing system achieving an average F1 score of approx. 0.86 across ROUGE-1, ROUGE-L, and BertScore metrics. Participating teams explored various fine-tuning and prompting strategies, as well as augmenting the SciVQA dataset with out-of-domain data and incorporating relevant context from source publications. The findings indicate that while MLLMs demonstrate strong performance on SciVQA, they face challenges in visual reasoning and still fall behind human judgments.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文概述了作为第五届学术文档处理研讨会 (SDP 2025) 一部分进行的首次科学视觉问答 (SciVQA) 共享任务。 SciVQA 旨在探索当前多模态大语言模型 (MLLM) 对学术出版物中的数据进行推理以进行问答 (QA) 的能力。挑战的主要焦点是封闭式视觉和非视觉 QA 对。我们开发了新颖的 SciVQA 基准，包含 3,000 张人物图像和总共 21,000 个 QA 对。该共享任务收到了 7 份提交，其中表现最好的系统的平均 F1 分数约为 1。 ROUGE-1、ROUGE-L 和 BertScore 指标均为 0.86。参与团队探索了各种微调和提示策略，以及使用域外数据增强 SciVQA 数据集并纳入源出版物中的相关上下文。研究结果表明，虽然 MLLM 在 SciVQA 上表现出强大的性能，但它们在视觉推理方面面临挑战，并且仍然落后于人类判断。

---

## 246. Visual Question Answering on Scientific Charts Using Fine-Tuned Vision-Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-SDP`

**作者**: Florian Schleid, Jan Strich, Chris Biemann

**ID**: 2025.sdp-1.19

**摘要(英文)**:
> Scientific charts often encapsulate the core findings of research papers, making the ability to answer questions about these charts highly valuable. This paper explores recent advancements in scientific chart visual question answering (VQA) enabled by large Vision Language Models (VLMs) and newly curated datasets. As part of the SciVQA shared task from the 5th Workshop on Scholarly Document Processing, we develop and evaluate multimodal Systems capable of answering diverse question types - including multiple-choice, yes/no, unanswerable, and infinite answer set questions - based on chart images extracted from scientific literature. We investigate the effects of zero-shot and one-shot prompting, as well as supervised fine-tuning (SFT), on the performance of Qwen2.5-VL models (7B and 32B variants). We also tried to include more training data from domain-specific datasets (SpiQA and ArXivQA). Our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL 32B model achieves a substantial improvement over the GPT-4o-mini baseline and reaches the 4th place in the shared task, highlighting the effectiveness of domain-specific fine-tuning. We published the code for the experiments.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 科学图表通常概括了研究论文的核心发现，使得回答有关这些图表的问题的能力非常有价值。本文探讨了大型视觉语言模型 (VLM) 和新整理的数据集在科学图表视觉问答 (VQA) 方面取得的最新进展。作为第五届学术文档处理研讨会 SciVQA 共享任务的一部分，我们开发和评估能够基于从科学文献中提取的图表图像回答不同问题类型（包括多项选择、是/否、无法回答和无限答案集问题）的多模态系统。我们研究了零样本和单样本提示以及监督微调 (SFT) 对 Qwen2.5-VL 模型（7B 和 32B 变体）性能的影响。我们还尝试包含来自特定领域数据集（SpiQA 和 ArXivQA）的更多训练数据。我们经过微调的 Qwen2.5-VL 32B 模型比 GPT-4o-mini 基线有了实质性的改进，并在共享任务中达到了第四名，凸显了特定领域微调的有效性。我们发布了实验代码。

---

## 247. Coling-UniAatSciVQA2025: Few-Shot Example Retrieval and Confidence-Informed Ensembling for Multimodal Large Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-SDP`

**作者**: Christian Jaumann, Annemarie Friedrich, Rainer Lienhart

**ID**: 2025.sdp-1.21

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper describes our system for the SciVQA 2025 Shared Task on Scientific Visual Question Answering. Our system employs an ensemble of two Multimodal Large Language Models and various few-shot example retrieval strategies. The model and few-shot setting are selected based on the figure and question type. We also select answers based on the models’ confidence levels. On the blind test data, our system ranks third out of seven with an average F1 score of 85.12 across ROUGE-1, ROUGE-L, and BERTS. Our code is publicly available.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文介绍了我们用于 SciVQA 2025 科学视觉问答共享任务的系统。我们的系统采用了两个多模态大语言模型和各种少镜头示例检索策略的集合。根据图形和问题类型选择模型和少样本设置。我们还根据模型的置信水平选择答案。在盲测数据上，我们的系统在 ROUGE-1、ROUGE-L 和 BERTS 中的平均 F1 分数为 85.12，在七个系统中排名第三。我们的代码是公开的。

---

## 248. Instruction-tunedQwenChart for Chart Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-SDP`

**作者**: Viviana Ventura, Lukas Amadeus Kleybolte, Alessandra Zarcone

**ID**: 2025.sdp-1.22

**摘要(英文)**:
> Charts, where information is delivered holistically by visual and textual features, represent a challenge when it comes to downstream tasks such as chart question answering, where both kinds of information contribute to the task. The standard approach is to decouple the task in two steps, first extracting information from the charts, or representing it as a table, text or code, and then a second reasoning step to output the answers. Today, the advancements in visual encoding of Visual Large Language Models (VLLM) have shown their capabilities to solve such complex tasks without using in-between representations of the charts or massive in-domain training. Our new instruction fine-tuned and chain-of-thought model QwenChart showed that even in a complex new benchmark such as SciVQA general models can achieve great performances with low-cost training, matching the capabilities that LLMs have showed in unimodal downstream tasks. An out-of-domain evaluation showed satisfactory results, albeit with an expected drop in performance.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 图表中的信息通过视觉和文本特征整体传递，这对于下游任务（例如图表问答）来说是一个挑战，其中两种信息都对任务有贡献。标准方法是将任务分两步解耦，首先从图表中提取信息，或将其表示为表格、文本或代码，然后进行第二个推理步骤以输出答案。如今，视觉大型语言模型 (VLLM) 视觉编码的进步已经显示出其解决此类复杂任务的能力，而无需使用图表的中间表示或大规模的域内训练。我们的新指令微调和思维链模型 QwenChart 表明，即使在 SciVQA 等复杂的新基准中，通用模型也可以通过低成本训练实现出色的性能，与大语言模型在单峰下游任务中表现出的能力相匹配。域外评估显示了令人满意的结果，尽管性能预期有所下降。

---

## 249. Enhancing Scientific Visual Question Answering through Multimodal Reasoning and Ensemble Modeling

**类别**: `Workshop-SDP`

**作者**: Prahitha Movva, Naga Harshita Marupaka

**ID**: 2025.sdp-1.23

**摘要(英文)**:
> Scholarly articles convey valuable information not only through unstructured text but also via (semi-)structured figures such as charts and diagrams. Automatically interpreting the semantics of knowledge encoded in these figures can be beneficial for downstream tasks such as question answering (QA). Current approaches to visual question answering often struggle with the precision required for scientific data interpretation, particularly in handling numerical values, multi-step reasoning over visual elements, and maintaining consistency between visual observation and textual reasoning. We present our approach to the SciVQA 2025 shared task, focusing on answering visual and non-visual questions grounded in scientific figures from scholarly articles.Our strongest individual model, InternVL3, achieved ROUGE-1 and ROUGE-L F1 scores of0.740and a BERTScore of0.983on the SciVQA test split. We also developed an ensemble model with multiple multimodal small language models (MSLMs). Through error analysis on the validation split, our ensemble approach achieves significant improvements over individual models and achieved ROUGE-1 and ROUGE-L F1 scores of0.735and0.734, respectively, and a BERTScore of0.979on the SciVQA test split. Our findings underscore the effectiveness of prompt optimization, chain-of-thought reasoning and ensemble modeling in improving the model’s ability in visual question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 学术文章不仅通过非结构化文本传达有价值的信息，还通过（半）结构化图形（例如图表和图表）传达有价值的信息。自动解释这些图中编码的知识的语义对于下游任务（例如问答（QA））是有益的。当前的视觉问答方法常常难以满足科学数据解释所需的精度，特别是在处理数值、对视觉元素的多步推理以及保持视觉观察和文本推理之间的一致性方面。我们介绍了 SciVQA 2025 共享任务的方法，重点是回答基于学术文章中的科学数据的视觉和非视觉问题。我们最强的个体模型 InternVL3 在 SciVQA 测试中获得了 0.740 的 ROUGE-1 和 ROUGE-L F1 分数以及 0.983 的 BERTScore。我们还开发了一个具有多个多模态小语言模型（MSLM）的集成模型。通过对验证分割的误差分析，我们的集成方法比各个模型取得了显着的改进，并分别获得了 0.735 和 0.734 的 ROUGE-1 和 ROUGE-L F1 分数，以及在 SciVQA 测试分割上的 BERTScore 0.979。我们的研究结果强调了即时优化、思维链推理和集成建模在提高模型视觉问答能力方面的有效性。

---

## 250. REFINDatSemEval-2025 Task 3: Retrieval-Augmented Factuality Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: DongGeon Lee, Hwanjo Yu

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.2

**摘要(英文)**:
> Hallucinations in large language model (LLM) outputs severely limit their reliability in knowledge-intensive tasks such as question answering. To address this challenge, we introduce REFIND (Retrieval-augmented Factuality hallucINation Detection), a novel framework that detects hallucinated spans within LLM outputs by directly leveraging retrieved documents. As part of the REFIND, we propose theContext Sensitivity Ratio (CSR), a novel metric that quantifies the sensitivity of LLM outputs to retrieved evidence. This innovative approach enables REFIND to efficiently and accurately detect hallucinations, setting it apart from existing methods. In the evaluation, REFIND demonstrated robustness across nine languages, including low-resource settings, and significantly outperformed baseline models, achieving superior IoU scores in identifying hallucinated spans. This work highlights the effectiveness of quantifying context sensitivity for hallucination detection, thereby paving the way for more reliable and trustworthy LLM applications across diverse languages. Our code is available athttps://github.com/oneonlee/REFIND.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 大语言模型（LLM）输出中的幻觉严重限制了它们在回答问题等知识密集型任务中的可靠性。为了应对这一挑战，我们引入了 REFIND（检索增强事实幻觉检测），这是一种新颖的框架，可通过直接利用检索到的文档来检测 LLM 输出中的幻觉跨度。作为 REFIND 的一部分，我们提出了上下文敏感性比 (CSR)，这是一种量化 LLM 输出对检索到的证据的敏感性的新颖指标。这种创新方法使 REFIND 能够高效、准确地检测幻觉，使其与现有方法区分开来。在评估中，REFIND 展示了跨九种语言（包括低资源环境）的稳健性，并且显着优于基线模型，在识别幻觉跨度方面取得了优异的 IoU 分数。这项工作强调了量化幻觉检测的上下文敏感性的有效性，从而为跨不同语言的更可靠和值得信赖的大语言模型应用铺平了道路。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/oneonlee/REFIND 获取。

---

## 251. I2R-NLPatSemEval-2025 Task 8: Question Answering on Tabular Data

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Yuze Gao, Bin Chen, Jian Su

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.14

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present a Large Language Model (LLM) based system for question answering (QA) over tabular data that leverages multi-turn prompting to automatically generate executable Pandas functions. Our framework decomposes the problem into three key steps: (1) Answer Type Identification, where the system identifies the expected format of the response (e.g., boolean, number, category); (2) Pandas Function Generation, which generates a corresponding Pandas function using table metadata and in-context examples, and (3) Error Correction and Regeneration, where iteratively refining the function based on error feedback from executions. Evaluations on the SemEval-2025 Task 8 Tabular QA benchmark (Grijalba et al., 2024) demonstrate that our multi-turn approach significantly outperforms single-turn prompting models in exact match accuracy by 7.3%. The proposed system not only improves code generation robustness but also paves the way for enhanced and adaptability in table-QA reasoning tasks. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/Gyyz/Question_Answering-over-Tabular-Data.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们提出了一个基于大型语言模型 (LLM) 的系统，用于通过表格数据进行问答 (QA)，该系统利用多轮提示自动生成可执行的 Pandas 函数。我们的框架将问题分解为三个关键步骤：（1）答案类型识别，系统识别响应的预期格式（例如布尔值、数字、类别）； (2) Pandas 函数生成，使用表元数据和上下文示例生成相应的 Pandas 函数；(3) 错误纠正和重新生成，根据执行的错误反馈迭代地细化函数。对 SemEval-2025 Task 8 Tabular QA 基准（Grijalba 等人，2024）的评估表明，我们的多轮方法在精确匹配准确率方面明显优于单轮提示模型 7.3%。所提出的系统不仅提高了代码生成的鲁棒性，而且还为表 QA 推理任务的增强和适应性铺平了道路。我们的实现可在 https://github.com/Gyyz/Question_Answering-over-Tabular-Data 获取。

---

## 252. 111DUTatSemEval-2025 Task 8:Hierarchical Chain-of-Thought Reasoning and Multi-Model Deliberation for RobustTableQA

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Jiaqi Yao, Erchen Yu, Yicen Tian, Yiyang Kang, Jiayi Zhang, Hongfei Lin, Linlin Zong, Bo Xu

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.32

**摘要(英文)**:
> The proliferation of structured tabular data in domains like healthcare and finance has intensified the demand for precise table question answering, particularly for complex numerical reasoning and cross-domain generalization. Existing approaches struggle with implicit semantics and multi-step arithmetic operations. This paper presents our solution for SemEval-2025 task,including three synergistic components: (1) a Schema Profiler that extracts structural metadata via LLM-driven analysis and statistical validation, (2) a Hierarchical Chain-of-Thought module that decomposes questions into four stages(semantic anchoring, schema mapping, query synthesis, and self-correction)to ensure SQL validity, and (3) a Confidence-Accuracy Voting mechanism that resolves discrepancies across LLMs through weighted ensemble decisions. Our framework achieves scores of 81.23 on Databench and 81.99 on Databench_lite, ranking 6th and 5th respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured metadata guidance and cross-model deliberation in complex TableQA scenarios.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 医疗保健和金融等领域中结构化表格数据的激增加剧了对精确表格问答的需求，特别是对于复杂的数字推理和跨领域泛化。现有方法难以应对隐式语义和多步算术运算。本文介绍了我们针对 SemEval-2025 任务的解决方案，包括三个协同组件：（1）通过 LLM 驱动的分析和统计验证提取结构元数据的模式分析器，（2）将问题分解为四个阶段（语义锚定、模式映射、查询合成和自我纠正）以确保 SQL 有效性的分层思维链模块，以及（3）解决问题的置信度-准确性投票机制通过加权整体决策来确定大语言模型之间的差异。我们的框架在 Databench 上获得了 81.23 分，在 Databench_lite 上获得了 81.99 分，分别排名第 6 和第 5，这证明了结构化元数据指导和跨模型审议在复杂 TableQA 场景中的有效性。

---

## 253. CCNUatSemEval-2025 Task 3: Leveraging Internal and External Knowledge of Large Language Models for Multilingual Hallucination Annotation

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Xu Liu, Guanyi Chen

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.62

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present the system developed by the Central China Normal University (CCNU) team for the Mu-SHROOM shared task, which focuses on identifying hallucinations in question-answering systems across 14 different languages. Our approach leverages multiple Large Language Models (LLMs) with distinct areas of expertise, employing them in parallel to annotate hallucinations, effectively simulating a crowdsourcing annotation process. Furthermore, each LLM-based annotator integrates both internal and external knowledge related to the input during the annotation process. Using the open-source LLM DeepSeek-V3, our system achieves the top ranking (#1) for Hindi data and secures a Top-5 position in seven other languages. In this paper, we also discuss unsuccessful approaches explored during our development process and share key insights gained from participating in this shared task.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们展示了华中师范大学 (CCNU) 团队为 Mu-SHROOM 共享任务开发的系统，该系统专注于识别 14 种不同语言的问答系统中的幻觉。我们的方法利用具有不同专业领域的多个大型语言模型（LLM），并行使用它们来注释幻觉，有效地模拟众包注释过程。此外，每个基于大语言模型的注释器都集成了注释过程中与输入相关的内部和外部知识。使用开源 LLM DeepSeek-V3，我们的系统在印地语数据方面取得了最高排名 (#1)，并在其他七种语言中获得了前 5 名的位置。在本文中，我们还讨论了在开发过程中探索的不成功方法，并分享了参与这项共同任务时获得的关键见解。

---

## 254. TableWise atSemEval-2025 Task 8:LLMAgents forTabQA

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Harsh Bansal, Aman Raj, Akshit Sharma, Parameswari Krishnamurthy

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.87

**摘要(英文)**:
> Tabular Question Answering (TabQA) is a challenging task that requires models to comprehend structured tabular data and generate accurate responses based on complex reasoning. In this paper, we present our approach to SemEval Task 8: Tabular Question Answering, where we develop a large language model (LLM)-based agent capable of understanding and reasoning over tabular inputs. Our agent leverages a hybrid retrieval and generation strategy, incorporating structured table parsing, semantic understanding, and reasoning mechanisms to enhance response accuracy. We fine-tune a pre-trained LLM on domain-specific tabular data, integrating chain-of-thought prompting and adaptive decoding to improve multi-hop reasoning over tables. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive performance, effectively handling numerical operations, entity linking, and logical inference. Our findings suggest that LLM-based agents, when properly adapted, can significantly advance the state of the art in tabular question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 表格问答 (TabQA) 是一项具有挑战性的任务，需要模型理解结构化表格数据并根据复杂推理生成准确的响应。在本文中，我们介绍了 SemEval 任务 8：表格问答的方法，其中我们开发了一个基于大型语言模型 (LLM) 的代理，能够理解和推理表格输入。我们的代理利用混合检索和生成策略，结合结构化表解析、语义理解和推理机制来提高响应准确性。我们针对特定领域的表格数据对预训练的 LLM 进行微调，集成思维链提示和自适应解码，以改进表格的多跳推理。实验结果表明，我们的方法实现了具有竞争力的性能，有效地处理数值运算、实体链接和逻辑推理。我们的研究结果表明，基于 LLM 的代理如果适当调整，可以显着提高表格问答的技术水平。

---

## 255. SBU-NLPatSemEval-2025 Task 8: Self-Correction and Collaboration inLLMs for Tabular Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Rashin Rahnamoun, Mehrnoush Shamsfard

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.97

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper explains the submission of the SBU-NLP team at SemEval-2025 Task 8: question-answering over tabular data. We present a novel algorithm for this task, aimed at systems capable of interpreting large tables and providing accurate answers to natural language queries. The evaluation uses the DataBench dataset, which covers a wide range of topics and reflects the complexity of real-world tabular data. Our approach incorporates a self-correction mechanism that iteratively refines LLM-generated code to address errors and prevent common mistakes. Additionally, a multi-LLM collaborative strategy is employed to generate answers, where responses from multiple LLMs are compared, and the majority consensus or a valid alternative is selected. The method relies exclusively on open-source models, avoiding costly processes like training or fine-tuning. Experimental results demonstrate that combining multiple LLMs with self-correction leads to significant performance improvements. However, challenges arise with list-based answers and responses involving multiple numerical, string, or boolean values, where further refinement is needed. The proposed simple system was among the top performers in both Subtask A and Subtask B among open-source models in the competition.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文解释了 SBU-NLP 团队在 SemEval-2025 任务 8：针对表格数据的问答中提交的内容。我们针对此任务提出了一种新颖的算法，针对能够解释大型表并为自然语言查询提供准确答案的系统。评估使用DataBench数据集，该数据集涵盖广泛的主题并反映了现实世界表格数据的复杂性。我们的方法采用了自我纠正机制，可以迭代地改进 LLM 生成的代码，以解决错误并防止常见错误。此外，采用多大语言模型协作策略来生成答案，对多个大语言模型的回答进行比较，并选择多数共识或有效替代方案。该方法完全依赖于开源模型，避免了训练或微调等成本高昂的过程。实验结果表明，将多个大语言模型与自我校正相结合可以显着提高性能。然而，基于列表的答案和涉及多个数字、字符串或布尔值的响应会带来挑战，需要进一步细化。所提出的简单系统在竞赛的开源模型中的子任务 A 和子任务 B 中均名列前茅。

---

## 256. CCNUatSemEval-2025 Task 8: Enhancing Question Answering on Tabular Data with Two-Stage Corrections

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Chenlian Zhou, Xilu Cai, Yajuan Tong, Chengzhao Wu, Xin Xu, Guanyi Chen, Tingting He

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.115

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present the system developed by the Central China Normal University (CCNU) team for the SemEval-2025 shared task 8, which focuses on Question-Answering (QA) for tabular data. Our approach leverages multiple Large Language Models (LLMs), conducting tabular QA as code completion. Additionally, to improve its reliability, we introduce a two-stage corrections mechanism, in which we instruct the LLM to correct the code according to the judges of whether the code is executable and whether the answer obtained from executing the code is semantically consistent with the question. The experiment demonstrates that code correction works but answer correction does not. Finally, we discuss other unsuccessful approaches explored during our development process.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们展示了华中师范大学 (CCNU) 团队为 SemEval-2025 共享任务 8 开发的系统，该系统重点关注表格数据的问答 (QA)。我们的方法利用多个大型语言模型 (LLM)，进行表格 QA 作为代码补全。此外，为了提高其可靠性，我们引入了两阶段纠正机制，根据代码是否可执行以及执行代码得到的答案在语义上是否与问题一致的判断来指示LLM纠正代码。实验表明，代码修正有效，但答案修正无效。最后，我们讨论在我们的开发过程中探索的其他不成功的方法。

---

## 257. AlexNLP-MOatSemEval-2025 Task 8: A Chain of Thought Framework for Question-Answering over Tabular Data

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Omar Mokhtar, Minah Ghanem, Nagwa El - Makky

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.118

**摘要(英文)**:
> Table Question Answering (TQA) involves extracting answers from structured data using natural language queries, a challenging task due to diverse table formats and complex reasoning. This work develops a TQA system using the DataBench dataset, leveraging large language models (LLMs) to generate Python code in a zero-shot manner. Our approach is highly generic, relying on a structured Chain-of-Thought framework to improve reasoning and data interpretation. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves high accuracy and efficiency, making it a flexible and effective solution for real-world tabular question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 表问答 (TQA) 涉及使用自然语言查询从结构化数据中提取答案，由于表格式多样且推理复杂，这是一项具有挑战性的任务。这项工作使用 DataBench 数据集开发了一个 TQA 系统，利用大型语言模型 (LLM) 以零样本的方式生成 Python 代码。我们的方法是高度通用的，依靠结构化的思维链框架来改进推理和数据解释。实验结果表明，我们的方法具有较高的准确性和效率，使其成为现实世界表格问答的灵活有效的解决方案。

---

## 258. ATLANTISatSemEval-2025 Task 3 : Detecting Hallucinated Text Spans in Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Catherine Kobus, Francois Lancelot, Marion - Cecile Martin, Nawal Ould Amer

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.145

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper presents the contributions of the ATLANTIS team to SemEval-2025 Task 3, focusing on detecting hallucinated text spans in question answering systems. Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced Natural Language Generation (NLG) but remain susceptible to hallucinations, generating incorrect or misleading content. To address this, we explored methods both with and without external context, utilizing few-shot prompting with a LLM, token-level classification or LLM fine-tuned on synthetic data. Notably, our approaches achieved top rankings in Spanish and competitive placements in English and German. This work highlights the importance of integrating relevant context to mitigate hallucinations and demonstrate the potential of fine-tuned models and prompt engineering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文介绍了 ATLANTIS 团队对 SemEval-2025 任务 3 的贡献，重点是检测问答系统中的幻觉文本跨度。大型语言模型 (LLM) 显着改进了自然语言生成 (NLG)，但仍然容易产生幻觉，生成不正确或误导性的内容。为了解决这个问题，我们探索了有和没有外部上下文的方法，利用 LLM 的小样本提示、标记级分类或对合成数据进行微调的 LLM。值得注意的是，我们的方法在西班牙语方面取得了最高排名，在英语和德语方面取得了有竞争力的排名。这项工作强调了整合相关背景以减轻幻觉的重要性，并展示了微调模型和即时工程的潜力。

---

## 259. Saama Technologies atSemEval-2025 Task 8: Few-shot prompting withLLM-generated examples for question answering on tabular data

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Kamal Raj Kanakarajan, Hwanmun Kim, Malaikannan Sankarasubbu

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.165

**摘要(英文)**:
> For SemEval 2025 Task 8, addressing tabular data question answering, we introduce a novel few-shot prompting system that guides large language models (LLMs) to generate Python code representing the reasoning process. Our system automatically creates a library of exemplar code snippets from training data, which are then used for few-shot prompting. Crucially, we incorporate a selection prompt to choose the best candidate code from multiple LLM-generated options, improving robustness and accuracy. Our system achieved competitive results, ranking 17th in the Open Model track and 25th overall. Ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our exemplar generation and code selection strategies. We conclude with a discussion of limitations and promising avenues for future research.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 对于 SemEval 2025 任务 8，解决表格数据问答问题，我们引入了一种新颖的小样本提示系统，该系统指导大型语言模型 (LLM) 生成代表推理过程的 Python 代码。我们的系统会根据训练数据自动创建示例代码片段库，然后将其用于少量提示。至关重要的是，我们纳入了一个选择提示，可以从多个 LLM 生成的选项中选择最佳候选代码，从而提高稳健性和准确性。我们的系统取得了有竞争力的成绩，在开放模型赛道中排名第 17 位，总体排名第 25 位。消融研究证明了我们的示例生成和代码选择策略的有效性。最后我们讨论了未来研究的局限性和有希望的途径。

---

## 260. Dataground atSemEval-2025 Task 8: SmallLLMs and Preference Optimization for TabularQA

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Giuseppe Attardi, Andrea Nelson Mauro, Daniele Sartiano

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.173

**摘要(英文)**:
> We present our submission to SemEval 2025 Task 8: Question Answering on Tabular Data, which challenges participants to develop systems capable of answering natural language questions on real-world tabular datasets. Our approach aims at generating Pandas code that can be run on such datasets to produce the desired answer. The approach consists in fine-tuning a Small Language Model (SLM) through Preference Optimization on both positive and negative examples generated by a teacher model.A base SLM is first elicited to produce the code to compute the answer to a question through a Chain of Thought (CoT) prompt. We performed extensive testing on the DataBench development set, exploring a variety of prompts, eventually settling on a detailed instruction prompt, followed by two-shot examples. Due to hardware constraints, the base model was an SLM with ${leq}$ 8 billion parameters.We then fine-tuned the model through Odds Ratio Preference Optimization (ORPO) using as training data the code produced by a teacher model on the DataBench training set. The teacher model was GPT-4o, whose code was labeled preferred, while the code generated by the base model was rejected. This increased the accuracy on the development set from 71% to 85%.Our method demonstrated robust performance in answering complex questions across diverse datasets, highlighting the effectiveness of combining small LLMs with supervised fine-tuning and automated code execution for tabular question answering.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 我们提交了 SemEval 2025 任务 8：表格数据问答，该挑战要求参与者开发能够回答现实世界表格数据集上的自然语言问题的系统。我们的方法旨在生成可以在此类数据集上运行的 Pandas 代码，以产生所需的答案。该方法包括通过对教师模型生成的正例和负例的偏好优化来微调小语言模型 (SLM)。首先引出基础 SLM 来生成代码，通过思维链 (CoT) 提示来计算问题的答案。我们在 DataBench 开发集上进行了广泛的测试，探索了各种提示，最终确定了详细的指令提示，然后是两次示例。由于硬件限制，基本模型是具有 ${leq}$ 80 亿参数的 SLM。然后，我们使用 DataBench 训练集上教师模型生成的代码作为训练数据，通过优势比偏好优化 (ORPO) 微调模型。教师模型是 GPT-4o，其代码被标记为首选，而基础模型生成的代码被拒绝。这将开发集的准确性从 71% 提高到 85%。我们的方法在回答不同数据集的复杂问题方面表现出了强大的性能，突出了将小型 LLM 与表格问答的监督微调和自动代码执行相结合的有效性。

---

## 261. Core Intelligence atSemEval-2025 Task 8: Multi-hopLLMAgent for Tabular Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Maryna Chernyshevich

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.174

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper describes a multi-hop LLM agent for tabular question answering developed for SemEval-2025 Task 8 and ranked 6th with 87% accuracy. Our approach combines proprietary LLM (ChatGPT-3.5-turbo) for code generation and open source LLM (Llama-3.2-3B) for answer validation.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文描述了为 SemEval-2025 Task 8 开发的用于表格问答的多跳 LLM 代理，并以 87% 的准确率排名第六。我们的方法结合了用于代码生成的专有 LLM (ChatGPT-3.5-turbo) 和用于答案验证的开源 LLM (Llama-3.2-3B)。

---

## 262. QleverAnswering-PUCRSatSemEval-2025 Task 8: ExploringLLMagents, code generation and correction for Table Question Answering

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: André Bergmann Lisboa, Lucas Cardoso Azevedo, Lucas Rafael Costella Pessutto

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.179

**摘要(英文)**:
> Table Question Answering (TQA) is a challenging task that requires reasoning over structured data to extract accurate answers. This paper presents QleverAnswering-PUCRS, our submission to SemEval-2025 Task 8: DataBench, Question-Answering over Tabular Data. QleverAnswering-PUCRS is a modular multi-agent system that employs a structured approach to TQA. The approach revolves around breaking down the task into specialized agents, each dedicated to handling a specific aspect of the problem. Our system was evaluated on benchmark datasets and achieved competitive results, ranking mid-to-top positions in the SemEval-2025 competition. Despite these promising results, we identify areas for improvement, particularly in handling complex queries and nested data structures.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 表问答 (TQA) 是一项具有挑战性的任务，需要对结构化数据进行推理以提取准确的答案。本文介绍了 QleverAnswering-PUCRS，这是我们提交给 SemEval-2025 任务 8：DataBench，表格数据问答的内容。 QleverAnswering-PUCRS 是一个模块化多代理系统，采用结构化方法进行 TQA。该方法围绕将任务分解为专门的代理，每个代理专门处理问题的特定方面。我们的系统在基准数据集上进行了评估，并取得了有竞争力的结果，在 SemEval-2025 竞赛中排名中上。尽管取得了这些令人鼓舞的结果，但我们还是确定了需要改进的领域，特别是在处理复杂查询和嵌套数据结构方面。

---

## 263. ITUNLPatSemEval-2025 Task 8: Question-Answering over Tabular Data: A Zero-Shot Approach usingLLM-Driven Code Generation

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Atakan Site, Emre Erdemir, Gülşen Eryiğit

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.198

**摘要(英文)**:
> This paper presents our system for SemEval-2025 Task 8: DataBench, Question-Answeringover Tabular Data. The primary objective ofthis task is to perform question answering ongiven tabular datasets from diverse domains;under two subtasks: DataBench QA (SubtaskI) and DataBench Lite QA (Subtask II). Totackle both subtasks, we developed a zero-shotsolution with a particular emphasis on lever-aging Large Language Model (LLM)-basedcode generation. Specifically, we proposeda Python code generation framework, utiliz-ing state-of-the-art open-source LLMs to gen-erate executable Pandas code via optimizedprompting strategies. Our experiments revealthat different LLMs exhibit varying levels ofeffectiveness in Python code generation. Addi-tionaly, results show that Python code genera-tion achieves superior performance in tabularquestion answering compared to alternative ap-proaches. Although our ranking among zero-shot systems is unknown at the time of this pa-per’s submission, our system achieved eighthplace in Subtask I and sixth place in Subtask IIamong the 30 systems that outperformed thebaseline in the open-source models category.

**摘要(中文)**:
> 本文介绍了我们用于 SemEval-2025 任务 8 的系统：DataBench，表格数据问答。此任务的主要目标是在两个子任务下对来自不同领域的给定表格数据集执行问答：DataBench QA（子任务 I）和 DataBench Lite QA（子任务 II）。为了解决这两个子任务，我们开发了一个零样本解决方案，特别强调利用基于大型语言模型 (LLM) 的代码生成。具体来说，我们提出了一个Python代码生成框架，利用最先进的开源LLM通过优化的提示策略生成可执行的Pandas代码。我们的实验表明，不同的大语言模型在 Python 代码生成方面表现出不同程度的有效性。此外，结果表明，与其他方法相比，Python 代码生成在表格问题回答中实现了卓越的性能。尽管我们在零样本系统中的排名在本文提交时尚不清楚，但我们的系统在开源模型类别中表现优于基线的 30 个系统中，在子任务 I 中排名第八，在子任务 II 中排名第六。

---

## 264. CSIROLTatSemEval-2025 Task 8: Answering Questions over Tabular Data usingLLMs

**类别**: `Workshop-SemEval`

**作者**: Tomas Turek, Shakila Mahjabin Tonni, Vincent Nguyen, Huichen Yang, Sarvnaz Karimi

**ID**: 2025.semeval-1.222

**摘要(英文)**:
> Question Answering over large tables is challenging due to the difficulty of reasoning required in linking information from different parts of a table, such as heading and metadata to the values in the table and information needs. We investigate using Large Language Models (LLM) for tabular reasoning, where, given a pair of a table and a question from the DataBench benchmark, the models generate answers.
